Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the ecology framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 807. The resulting operator achieves bound O(844/n^3).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by ecology generators. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 844. QED.
